## 15 Oct 2026 — synthetic PG18 fast-exit pg_dump stdout race ROOT CAUSE REPRODUCED; bounded capture FIX STAGED (NOT OWNER-RUN)

Owner tested candidate `d1cc5b917b812e3779766c5a17fa9b96c1555f29`: archive/unit/source security tests **5/5 PASS**, isolated Windows PG18 synthetic init/start/DB/fixture phases **00–05 PASS**, but actual encrypted `pg_dump` stream **FAIL**: `Synthetic pg_dump/archive failed (exit 0): Empty archive source stream; ... stderr: (empty)`; stage 10 PostgreSQL cleanup logged, without reported cleanup error. No production DB operation, no dump and no PG18 full restore PASS.

**Root cause independently reproduced using Node.js without PostgreSQL:** a fast-exiting child writes literal `PGDMP` to stdout; parent delays attaching reader for ~600ms (simulating `await scrypt(...)`); unbuffered child.stdout returned **empty**, while immediately `child.stdout.pipe(new PassThrough())` before delay recovered exact `PGDMP`. On Node child exit, unconsumed stdout may be drained before the encryption key derivation resolves. PostgreSQL docs explicitly specify that `pg_dump -Fc` without `-f` writes standard output; the source format flag was not itself wrong. This independently observed reproduction supports the race explanation, but full Windows PG18 export→restore remains unverified.

**Minimal implementation:** `tooling/v380-backup-archive.mjs` exports `retainChildStdout(child)`, synchronously piping child.stdout into a **64KiB bounded PassThrough**, preserving backpressure and forwarding stream errors without any plaintext files. Both `tooling/v380-off-neon-pg18-synthetic-test.mjs` and `tooling/v380-off-neon-export.mjs` attach this buffer IMMEDIATELY after spawning pg_dump, before any asynchronous `scrypt`/cipher setup, and encrypt from `bufferedDump`. Kept PGDMP signature guard, process exit=0+stderr diagnostics, AES-GCM authentication and isolated restore checks. Added **two regressions**: fast-exit child writes PGDMP during key derivation and source ordering in both callers. Existing tests updated for new safe stream path. Syntax and GitHub source readback **PARSED**, owner Windows tests after these changes **NOT RUN**. No real production dump/backup, Neon/DDL/merge/deploy; PR remains Draft/NO-GO.

```powershell

```
Expected target: seven security/source regressions PASS, actual PG18 stage 05 shows nonzero custom-format bytes, stage 06/07 authenticated decrypt/pg_restore, data comparison, 10/10 cleanup and final PASS. If not, halt and report exact phase/exit/stderr. Before first real production export require independent DeepSeek security review disposition; historical signed v13 recovery separate OPEN. Production stays unchanged.

## 15 Oct 2026 — synthetic PG18 stage 05 archive-size gate FAIL; narrowed validation staged, re-test PENDING

Owner ran checkout `c0d6f2d497b28cb773c0acb48984f27d184ca074` after cleaning three abandoned PG18 synthetic test folders (no synthetic `postgres.exe` remaining). Encrypted archive regressions **4/4 PASS**. The updated synthetic PostgreSQL18 integration reached **00–05/10**: tools, isolated PG18 init/start on loopback port 50207, creation of two fresh DBs, synthetic rows, then failed at cipher stream with `Encrypted database archive unexpectedly small.`. `10/10` stop/cleanup ran without a reported error; **synthetic end-to-end restore NOT PASS**. Never inferred production database involvement, as no Neon URL or prod data is used. All historical H4 and v3.8 local results unaffected.

**Root-cause constraint:** prior AES archive codec rejected all ciphertext <= header (36 bytes) + tag (16 bytes) + arbitrary 1024 payload bytes, **before** the caller checked the `pg_dump` exit status/stderr. Thus error may signify either a legitimate tiny compressed PG custom-format dump **or** an empty/failed `pg_dump`; no evidence yet to decide between them. Do not silently assume a valid dump or weaken integrity acceptance.

**Staged test-only/backup-tool minimal fix:** `v380-backup-archive.mjs` now validates a **nonempty plaintext stream** and optionally exact `PGDMP` first-five-byte signature across chunk boundaries while encrypting. It verifies cipher length equals authenticated header+tag+plaintext count instead of imposing arbitrary 1024 payload floor. Decrypt refuses zero-payload envelopes; AES-GCM tag validation remains mandatory, and only a successful actual PG18 `pg_restore` of the full archive will confirm dump validity. Both the production export caller and synthetic PG18 caller require `{requirePgDumpCustomFormat:true}`. Synthetic caller now captures actual `pg_dump` stderr/exit code and reports count of source bytes; production caller prioritizes real `pg_dump` failure diagnostics before finalizing any manifest. Added regression for tiny signed streams, empty/non-PGDMP rejection and diagnostic contract. **No new local runtime test yet**; GitHub source syntax readback PARSED. No production export/restore/DDL/merge/deploy.


## 2. Post-release order

**Documentation / AI-development governance — DONE (11 October 2026):** [123-file source inventory](docs/maintenance/2026-10-11-documentation-ai-workflow-audit.md), [independent DeepSeek review brief](docs/maintenance/2026-10-11-deepseek-ai-workflow-review-brief.md), [completed DeepSeek/source reconciliation](docs/maintenance/2026-10-11-deepseek-review-reconciliation.md) and [DOC-5 final closeout](docs/maintenance/2026-10-11-doc5-ai-governance-closeout.md) preserve the audit trail. **DOC-0–5 completed in merged PRs #312–#317; no documentation review remains a prerequisite for 3.8.0 discovery.** Follow-up independent review identified minor navigation/wording corrections, not a new product gate. No runtime, DB or Training changes. Frozen decisions: ROADMAP owns current release and next priority; FEATURES owns capabilities; VERSIONING owns numbering; CHANGELOG is append-only; development history stays verbatim; AGENTS remains pointer-only; documentation checker remains optional/report-only. Product release 3.7.0 remains closed, and 3.8.0 Currency (#136) is the next product milestone.

1. **3.8.0 Currency / monetary semantics (#136) — NEXT:** read actual `main`, define and review source-backed account/flight currency and migration/backup/export boundaries before implementation. No automatic exchange rates or silent historical conversion.
2. **External map-provider evidence — separate unresolved compliance work:** Esri/openAIP permissions, third-party attribution/export/Story rights, source currency/coverage, quotas and billing remain **UNVERIFIED**; not silently approved by 3.7.0 functional acceptance or the release label. Owner elected to defer Esri attribution work in this cycle. Do not expand provider usage or assert approved aviation chart status without applicable evidence.
3. **Operational production watch:** Vercel build and alias identity are verified. The owner had tested the unchanged map logic on 10 October; direct post-release browser/HTTP smoke and actual displayed footer screenshot remain **NOT RUN** from this environment, and any new issue should be triaged against the deployed SHA rather than assumed absent. No extra deployment or DB action implied.
4. **Historical milestones:** earlier 3.7.0 acceptance plan and superseded decisions remain preserved in [final acceptance](docs/product/3_7_0_FINAL_PRODUCTION_ACCEPTANCE_2026-10-11.md) and dated A2D/A2E/A2F reports; no abandoned Satellite R1/R2 revival.

## 3. Frozen decisions / constraints

- **Repos remain separate:** No FlyTally Training changes in Logbook work.
- **Maps:** Standard default; authenticated-only optional Satellite and Aviation; no silently persisted layer preference or automatic switch; public replay stays Standard-only. Share Story's older map behavior must not be altered by this docs effort.
- **Standard map API:** missing `style` = legacy `map`; exactly one `style=map` or `style=satellite` accepted. Duplicate/malformed/unknown/empty/case-variant style → HTTP 400 `unsupported_style`, `no-store` before upstream.
- **Satellite security:** server-side live session check and private/no-store Satellite response. Never expose upstream credential or substitute a client flag for authentication.
- **Aviation applicability:** owner selected **combined Aviation raster**, superseding the earlier airspaces-only label. The private layer is reference-only, never an approved aviation chart, live NOTAM, airspace activation or clearance authority. Client can retain native z14 imagery through display z18; server still only requests permitted z<=14.
- **Operational evidence:** Third-party permissions, map coverage/currentness, and real provider responses cannot be inferred from a merged PR, an enabled UI flag or a synthetic test.
- **Flights and compliance:** one canonical flight model; shared strict Create/Edit/GPS rules; category/configuration/evidence fail closed; certified/finalized records stay auditable. No unapproved regulatory or certification claims.
- **Engineering:** exact-SHA test provenance, local ≠ CI, build ≠ runtime. Database schema/backup changes need independent migration and deployment prerequisites. No production DB operations as part of docs cleanup.

### Historical M2A source audit — no R1/R2 revival planned

The [10 October exact-SHA source review](docs/maintenance/2026-10-10-r2-m2a-source-audit.md) identified technical differences in old PRs (#273 Story tile failure handling; #278 server-only emergency stop; #279 non-integrated resource controls; #281 provider geometry review). **Owner declined revival of these dead-end development paths on 11 October 2026.** These are **not current scheduled milestones, approval to port code, or proof of a production incident**. If a genuine problem is observed in the active app, characterize it against current main and reprioritize it separately. Continue the actual 3.7.0 provider/iPad/integrated acceptance gates; nothing here approves its full release.

## 6. Documentation ownership and release DoD

- `ROADMAP.md` = current decisions, order, dependencies, milestones and acceptance.
- `FEATURES.md` = implemented/planned capability truth and intentional limits.
- `CHANGELOG.md` = what changed, with dated historical test and production evidence.
- `ARCHITECTURE.md` = runtime, data and domain contracts; `DEVELOPMENT.md` = verification, branches, CI and deployment policy.
- `docs/product/` = supporting source-backed implementation and milestone acceptance; `docs/history/` = earlier evidence and verbatim snapshots; `docs/maintenance/` = one-time audits and housekeeping plans.

A milestone is **DONE** only after implementation, evidence-based verification, relevant data/deploy checks and the required docs have been reconciled. Historical status errors are corrected by new dated evidence, never by misrepresenting older failures.
